# 2027 New-Grad Timeline

Checked 2026-10-02. 1778 companies in `COMPANY_LIST.md`. Full data: `status.csv`.

- **Apply now:** 314 open, not yet applied: 199 in your areas, 115 elsewhere (53 with a stated deadline)
- **Referrals / cold messages now:** 228 expected to open by 2026-11-27 or overdue vs last year
- **Tracker leads to confirm:** 0 listed by a GitHub tracker, company board not checked yet
- **Later / unknown timing:** 303
- **Already in TRACKER.md:** 27
- **No US new-grad SWE track found:** 820
- **Dropped (citizenship, green card, or clearance required):** 87 — kept in `status.csv` as `excluded`

"Apply now" only counts postings seen on the company's own job board. GitHub trackers (SimplifyJobs, speedyapply, vanshb03) are used for leads and last-cycle dates. Workday dates are the latest repost.
